Mathlib.Topology.AlexandrovDiscrete

AlexandrovDiscreteclassMathlib.Topology.AlexandrovDiscrete, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $x$ be a set. Then we can say that $\mathrm{AlexandrovDiscrete}$ holds for $x$.
Formula
$x \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{AlexandrovDiscrete} (x)\ \mathsf{prop}$
French
Soit $x$ un ensemble. Alors nous pouvons dire que $\mathrm{AlexandrovDiscrete}$ tient pour $x$.

AffineSubspace.ofEqdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.AffineSubspace, function or constant
Wording
the affine subspace of h2 (from the name)
English
Assume that $\EqtMACRoH {s}{t}$. Then the affine subspace of $h_{2}$ is a continuous affine equivalence of $\SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {s}{x_{5}})}$ and $\SubtypeSet {x_{6}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {t}{x_{6}})}$.
Formula
$\EqtMACRoJ {s}{t}\vdash \operatorname{ofEq} (h_{2})\in \mathrm{ContinuousAffineEquiv} (R , \SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {s}{x_{5}})}, \SubtypeSet {x_{6}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {t}{x_{6}})})$
French
Supposons que $\EqtMACRoH {s}{t}$. Alors le sous-espace affine de $h_{2}$ est une équivalence affine continue de $\SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {s}{x_{5}})}$ et $\SubtypeSet {x_{6}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {t}{x_{6}})}$.
AffineSubspace.subtypeAdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.AffineSubspace, function or constant
Wording
the subtype of s (from the name)
English
Let $s$ be an affine subspace of $R$ and $P$. Then the subtype of $s$ is a continuous affine map of $\SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {s}{x_{5}})}$ and $P$.
Formula
$s \in \mathrm{AffineSubspace} (R , P)\vdash \operatorname{subtypeA} (s)\in \mathrm{ContinuousAffineMap} (R , \SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {s}{x_{5}})}, P)$
French
Soit $s$ un sous-espace affine de $R$ et $P$. Alors le sous-type de $s$ est une application affine continue de $\SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {s}{x_{5}})}$ et $P$.
ContinuousAffineEquiv.affineSubspaceMapdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.AffineSubspace, function or constant
Wording
the affine subspace map of e and s (from the name)
English
Let $e$ be a continuous affine equivalence of $P$ and $Q$. Let $s$ be an affine subspace of $R$ and $P$. Then the affine subspace map of $e$ and $s$ is a continuous affine equivalence of $\SubtypeSet {x_{9}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {s}{x_{9}})}$ and $\SubtypeSet {x_{10}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\operatorname{map} (\operatorname{toAffineMap} (\operatorname{toAffineEquiv} (e)), s)}{x_{10}})}$.
Formula
$e \in \mathrm{ContinuousAffineEquiv} (R , P , Q), s \in \mathrm{AffineSubspace} (R , P)\vdash \operatorname{affineSubspaceMap} (e , s)\in \mathrm{ContinuousAffineEquiv} (R , \SubtypeSet {x_{9}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {s}{x_{9}})}, \SubtypeSet {x_{10}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {\operatorname{map} (\operatorname{toAffineMap} (\operatorname{toAffineEquiv} (e)), s)}{x_{10}})})$
French
Soit $e$ une équivalence affine continue de $P$ et $Q$. Soit $s$ un sous-espace affine de $R$ et $P$. Alors l'application de sous-espaces affine de $e$ et $s$ est une équivalence affine continue de $\SubtypeSet {x_{9}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {s}{x_{9}})}$ et $\SubtypeSet {x_{10}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\operatorname{map} (\operatorname{toAffineMap} (\operatorname{toAffineEquiv} (e)), s)}{x_{10}})}$.

Mathlib.Topology.Algebra.Algebra

Algebra.elementaldefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Algebra, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $R$ be a set. Let $x_{4}$ be an element of $A$. Then $\operatorname{elemental}$ applied to $R$ and $x_{4}$ is a subalgebra of $R$ and $A$ defined as the topological closure of $\operatorname{adjoin}$ applied to $R$ and $\operatorname{singleton}$ applied to $x_{4}$.
Formula
$R \in \mathrm{Set} , x_{4} \in A \vdash \operatorname{elemental} (R , x_{4}):= \operatorname{topologicalClosure} (\operatorname{adjoin} (R , \operatorname{singleton} (x_{4})))\in \mathrm{Subalgebra} (R , A)$
French
Soit $R$ un ensemble. Soit $x_{4}$ un élément de $A$. Alors $\operatorname{elemental}$ appliqué à $R$ et $x_{4}$ est une sous-algèbre de $R$ et $A$ défini comme la clôture topologique de $\operatorname{adjoin}$ appliqué à $R$ et $\operatorname{singleton}$ appliqué à $x_{4}$.
ContinuousAlgHomstructureMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Algebra, type
Wording
continuous algebra homomorphism of A and B (from the name)
English
Let $R$, $A$ and $B$ be sets. Then the continuous algebra homomorphisms of $A$ and $B$ are a basic type.
Formula
$R , A , B \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{ContinuousAlgHom} (R , A , B)\ \mathsf{set}$
French
Soient $R$, $A$ et $B$ des ensembles. Alors les homomorphismes d'algèbres continus de $A$ et $B$ sont un type de base.
ContinuousAlgHom.codRestrictdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Algebra, function or constant
Wording
the codomain restriction of f and p (from the name, with words of the lexicon)
English
Let $f$ be a continuous algebra homomorphism of $A$ and $B$. Let $p$ be a subalgebra of $R$ and $B$ such that for all elements $x_{8}$ of $A$, $\MembershipmemMACRoT {p}{f (x_{8})}$. Then the codomain restriction of $f$ and $p$ is a continuous algebra homomorphism of $A$ and $\SubtypeSet {x_{7}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {p}{x_{7}})}$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{ContinuousAlgHom} (R , A , B), p \in \mathrm{Subalgebra} (R , B), \forall x_{8} \in A . \MembershipmemMACRoAA {p}{f (x_{8})}\vdash \operatorname{codRestrict} (f , p , h)\in \mathrm{ContinuousAlgHom} (R , A , \SubtypeSet {x_{7}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {p}{x_{7}})})$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme d'algèbres continu de $A$ et $B$. Soit $p$ une sous-algèbre de $R$ et $B$ telle que pour tous les éléments $x_{8}$ de $A$, $\MembershipmemMACRoT {p}{f (x_{8})}$. Alors la restriction de codomaines de $f$ et $p$ est un homomorphisme d'algèbres continu de $A$ et $\SubtypeSet {x_{7}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {p}{x_{7}})}$.
ContinuousAlgHom.compdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Algebra, function or constant
Wording
the composition of g and f (from the name)
English
Let $g$ be a continuous algebra homomorphism of $B$ and $C$. Let $f$ be a continuous algebra homomorphism of $A$ and $B$. Then the composition of $g$ and $f$ is a continuous algebra homomorphism of $A$ and $C$.
Formula
$g \in \mathrm{ContinuousAlgHom} (R , B , C), f \in \mathrm{ContinuousAlgHom} (R , A , B)\vdash \operatorname{comp} (g , f)\in \mathrm{ContinuousAlgHom} (R , A , C)$
French
Soit $g$ un homomorphisme d'algèbres continu de $B$ et $C$. Soit $f$ un homomorphisme d'algèbres continu de $A$ et $B$. Alors la composition de $g$ et $f$ est un homomorphisme d'algèbres continu de $A$ et $C$.
ContinuousAlgHom.copydefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Algebra, function or constant
Wording
the copy of f and h (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a continuous algebra homomorphism of $A$ and $B$. Let $\mathrm{f'}$ be a function from elements of $A$ to elements of $B$ such that $\mathrm{f'}$ is equal to $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $f$. Then the copy of $f$ and $\mathrm{f'}$ is a continuous algebra homomorphism of $A$ and $B$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{ContinuousAlgHom} (R , A , B), \mathrm{f'} \in A \rightarrow B , \EqtMACRoJ {\mathrm{f'}}{\operatorname{coe} (f)}\vdash \operatorname{copy} (f , \mathrm{f'} , h)\in \mathrm{ContinuousAlgHom} (R , A , B)$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme d'algèbres continu de $A$ et $B$. Soit $\mathrm{f'}$ une fonction des éléments de $A$ à des éléments de $B$ telle que $\mathrm{f'}$ soit égal à $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à $f$. Alors la copie de $f$ et $\mathrm{f'}$ est un homomorphisme d'algèbres continu de $A$ et $B$.
ContinuousAlgHom.fstdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Algebra, function or constant
Wording
the first component of A and B (from the name)
English
Let $R$, $A$ and $B$ be sets. Then the first component of $A$ and $B$ is a continuous algebra homomorphism of $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $A$ and $B$ and $A$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to the first component of $A$ and $B$ and $\mathrm{continuous\_fst}$.
Formula
$R , A , B \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{fst} (R , A , B):= \operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{fst} (R , A , B), \mathrm{continuous\_fst})\in \mathrm{ContinuousAlgHom} (R , \mathrm{Prod} (A , B), A)$
French
Soient $R$, $A$ et $B$ des ensembles. Alors la composante première de $A$ et $B$ est un homomorphisme d'algèbres continu de $\mathrm{Prod}$ appliqué à $A$ et $B$ et $A$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à la composante première de $A$ et $B$ et $\mathrm{continuous\_fst}$.

English

ContinuousAlgHom.proddefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Algebra, function or constant
Wording
the product of x10 and x11 (from the name)
English
Let $x_{10}$ be a continuous algebra homomorphism of $A$ and $B$. Let $x_{11}$ be a continuous algebra homomorphism of $A$ and $C$. Then the product of $x_{10}$ and $x_{11}$ is a continuous algebra homomorphism of $A$ and $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $B$ and $C$.
Formula
$x_{10} \in \mathrm{ContinuousAlgHom} (R , A , B), x_{11} \in \mathrm{ContinuousAlgHom} (R , A , C)\vdash \operatorname{prod} (x_{10} , x_{11})\in \mathrm{ContinuousAlgHom} (R , A , \mathrm{Prod} (B , C))$

ContinuousAlgHom.prodEquivdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Algebra, function or constant
Wording
the product equivalence (from the name)
English
The product equivalence is an equivalence from $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to the set of continuous algebra homomorphisms of $A$ and $B$ and the set of continuous algebra homomorphisms of $A$ and $C$ to the set of continuous algebra homomorphisms of $A$ and $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $B$ and $C$.
Formula
$\operatorname{prodEquiv} \in \EquivMACRoAL {\mathrm{Prod} (\mathrm{ContinuousAlgHom} (R , A , B), \mathrm{ContinuousAlgHom} (R , A , C))}{\mathrm{ContinuousAlgHom} (R , A , \mathrm{Prod} (B , C))}$
French
L'équivalence de produits est une équivalence de $\mathrm{Prod}$ appliqué à l'ensemble d'homomorphismes d'algèbres continus de $A$ et $B$ et l'ensemble d'homomorphismes d'algèbres continus de $A$ et $C$ à l'ensemble d'homomorphismes d'algèbres continus de $A$ et $\mathrm{Prod}$ appliqué à $B$ et $C$.
ContinuousAlgHom.prodMapdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Algebra, function or constant
Wording
the product map of x13 and x14 (from the name)
English
Let $x_{13}$ be a continuous algebra homomorphism of $A$ and $B$. Let $x_{14}$ be a continuous algebra homomorphism of $C$ and $D$. Then the product map of $x_{13}$ and $x_{14}$ is a continuous algebra homomorphism of $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $A$ and $C$ and $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $B$ and $D$ defined as the product of the composition of $x_{13}$ and the first component of $A$ and $C$ and the composition of $x_{14}$ and the second component of $A$ and $C$.
Formula
$x_{13} \in \mathrm{ContinuousAlgHom} (R , A , B), x_{14} \in \mathrm{ContinuousAlgHom} (R , C , D)\vdash \operatorname{prodMap} (x_{13} , x_{14}):= \operatorname{prod} (\operatorname{comp} (x_{13} , \operatorname{fst} (R , A , C)), \operatorname{comp} (x_{14} , \operatorname{snd} (R , A , C)))\in \mathrm{ContinuousAlgHom} (R , \mathrm{Prod} (A , C), \mathrm{Prod} (B , D))$
French
Soit $x_{13}$ un homomorphisme d'algèbres continu de $A$ et $B$. Soit $x_{14}$ un homomorphisme d'algèbres continu de $C$ et $D$. Alors l'application de produits de $x_{13}$ et $x_{14}$ est un homomorphisme d'algèbres continu de $\mathrm{Prod}$ appliqué à $A$ et $C$ et $\mathrm{Prod}$ appliqué à $B$ et $D$ défini comme le produit de la composition de $x_{13}$ et la composante première de $A$ et $C$ et la composition de $x_{14}$ et la composante seconde de $A$ et $C$.

AddEquiv.toContinuousAddEquivdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.ContinuousMonoidHom, function or constant
Wording
the continuous additive equivalence of e and he (from the name)
English
Let $e$ be an additive equivalence of $G$ and $H$ such that for all elements $s$ of $\wp H$, the preimage of $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $e$ and $s$ is open, if and only if $s$ is open. Then the continuous additive equivalence of $e$ and $\mathrm{he}$ is a continuous additive equivalence of $G$ and $H$.
Formula
$e \in \mathrm{AddEquiv} (G , H), \forall s \in \wp H . \mathrm{IsOpen} (\operatorname{preimage} (\operatorname{coe} (e), s))\leftrightarrow \mathrm{IsOpen} (s)\vdash \operatorname{toContinuousAddEquiv} (e , \mathrm{he})\in \mathrm{ContinuousAddEquiv} (G , H)$
French
Soit $e$ une équivalence additive de $G$ et $H$ telle que pour tous les éléments $s$ de $\wp H$, la préimage de $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à $e$ et $s$ soit ouverte si et seulement si $s$ est ouvert. Alors l'équivalence additive continue de $e$ et $\mathrm{he}$ est une équivalence additive continue de $G$ et $H$.

Let $E$ be a set. Then the sum of $E$ is a continuous additive monoid homomorphism of $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $E$ and $E$ and $E$.
Formula
$E \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{add} (E)\in \mathrm{ContinuousAddMonoidHom} (\mathrm{Prod} (E , E), E)$
French
Soit $E$ un ensemble. Alors la somme de $E$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif continu de $\mathrm{Prod}$ appliqué à $E$ et $E$ et $E$.
ContinuousAddMonoidHom.compdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.ContinuousMonoidHom, function or constant
Wording
the composition of g and f (from the name)
English
Let $g$ be a continuous additive monoid homomorphism of $B$ and $C$. Let $f$ be a continuous additive monoid homomorphism of $A$ and $B$. Then the composition of $g$ and $f$ is a continuous additive monoid homomorphism of $A$ and $C$.
Formula
$g \in \mathrm{ContinuousAddMonoidHom} (B , C), f \in \mathrm{ContinuousAddMonoidHom} (A , B)\vdash \operatorname{comp} (g , f)\in \mathrm{ContinuousAddMonoidHom} (A , C)$
French
Soit $g$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif continu de $B$ et $C$. Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif continu de $A$ et $B$. Alors la composition de $g$ et $f$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif continu de $A$ et $C$.
ContinuousAddMonoidHom.coproddefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.ContinuousMonoidHom, function or constant
Wording
the coproduct of f and g (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a continuous additive monoid homomorphism of $A$ and $E$. Let $g$ be a continuous additive monoid homomorphism of $B$ and $E$. Then the coproduct of $f$ and $g$ is a continuous additive monoid homomorphism of $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $A$ and $B$ and $E$ defined as the composition of the sum of $E$ and the product map of $f$ and $g$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{ContinuousAddMonoidHom} (A , E), g \in \mathrm{ContinuousAddMonoidHom} (B , E)\vdash \operatorname{coprod} (f , g):= \operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{add} (E), \operatorname{prodMap} (f , g))\in \mathrm{ContinuousAddMonoidHom} (\mathrm{Prod} (A , B), E)$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif continu de $A$ et $E$. Soit $g$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif continu de $B$ et $E$. Alors le coproduit de $f$ et $g$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif continu de $\mathrm{Prod}$ appliqué à $A$ et $B$ et $E$ défini comme la composition de la somme de $E$ et l'application de produits de $f$ et $g$.
ContinuousAddMonoidHom.diagdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.ContinuousMonoidHom, function or constant
Wording
the diagonal of A (from the name)
English
Let $A$ be a set. Then the diagonal of $A$ is a continuous additive monoid homomorphism of $A$ and $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $A$ and $A$ defined as the product of the identity continuous homomorphism of $A$ and the identity continuous homomorphism of $A$.
Formula
$A \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{diag} (A):= \operatorname{prod} (\operatorname{id} (A), \operatorname{id} (A))\in \mathrm{ContinuousAddMonoidHom} (A , \mathrm{Prod} (A , A))$
French

ContinuousAddMonoidHom.ofClassdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.ContinuousMonoidHom, function or constant
Wording
the continuous additive monoid homomorphism of f (from the name)
English
Let $A$, $B$ and $F$ be sets. Let $f$ be an element of $F$. Then the continuous additive monoid homomorphism of $f$ is a continuous additive monoid homomorphism of $A$ and $B$ defined as the continuous additive monoid homomorphism of $f$.
Formula
$A , B , F \in \mathrm{Set} , f \in F \vdash \operatorname{ofClass} (A , B , F , f):= \operatorname{toContinuousAddMonoidHom} (f)\in \mathrm{ContinuousAddMonoidHom} (A , B)$

Wording
the swap of A and B (from the name)
English

ContinuousAddMonoidHom.toContinuousMapdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.ContinuousMonoidHom, function or constant
Wording
the continuous map of self (from the name)
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be a continuous additive monoid homomorphism of $A$ and $B$. Then the continuous map of $\mathrm{self}$ is a continuous map of $A$ and $B$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $\operatorname{toFun}$ applied to $\operatorname{toZeroHom}$ applied to $\operatorname{toAddMonoidHom}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$ and $\mathrm{ContinuousAddMonoidHom\_\_continuous\_toFun}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{self} \in \mathrm{ContinuousAddMonoidHom} (A , B)\vdash \operatorname{toContinuousMap} (\mathrm{self}):= \operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{toFun} (\operatorname{toZeroHom} (\operatorname{toAddMonoidHom} (\mathrm{self}))), \mathrm{ContinuousAddMonoidHom\_\_continuous\_toFun} (\mathrm{self}))\in \mathrm{ContinuousMap} (A , B)$
French
Soit $\mathrm{self}$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif continu de $A$ et $B$. Alors l'application continue de $\mathrm{self}$ est une application continue de $A$ et $B$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toFun}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toZeroHom}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toAddMonoidHom}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$ et $\mathrm{ContinuousAddMonoidHom\_\_continuous\_toFun}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$.

ClosedAddSubgroupstructureMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Group.ClosedSubgroup, type
Wording
closed additive subgroup of G (from the name)
English
Let $G$ be a set. Then the closed additive subgroups of $G$ are a basic type.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{ClosedAddSubgroup} (G)\ \mathsf{set}$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors les sous-groupes additifs fermés de $G$ sont un type de base.

Mathlib.Topology.Algebra.Group.CompactOpen

ContinuousAddMonoidHom.compLeftdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Group.CompactOpen, function or constant
Wording
the left composition of E and f (from the name)
English
Let $E$ be a set. Let $f$ be a continuous additive monoid homomorphism of $A$ and $B$. Then the left composition of $E$ and $f$ is a continuous additive monoid homomorphism of the set of continuous additive monoid homomorphisms of $B$ and $E$ and the set of continuous additive monoid homomorphisms of $A$ and $E$.
Formula
$E \in \mathrm{Set} , f \in \mathrm{ContinuousAddMonoidHom} (A , B)\vdash \operatorname{compLeft} (E , f)\in \mathrm{ContinuousAddMonoidHom} (\mathrm{ContinuousAddMonoidHom} (B , E), \mathrm{ContinuousAddMonoidHom} (A , E))$
French
Soit $E$ un ensemble. Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif continu de $A$ et $B$. Alors la composition gauche de $E$ et $f$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif continu de l'ensemble d'homomorphismes de monoïdes additifs continus de $B$ et $E$ et l'ensemble d'homomorphismes de monoïdes additifs continus de $A$ et $E$.

AddGroupTopology.coinduceddefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Group.GroupTopology, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $f$ be a function from elements of $x$ to elements of $x_{1}$. Then $\operatorname{coinduced}$ applied to $f$ is an additive group topology of $x_{1}$ defined as the infimum of $\operatorname{ofPred}$ applied to the function that maps $b$ to $\mathrm{Leq} (\operatorname{coinduced} (f , t), \operatorname{toTopologicalSpace} (b))$.
Formula
$f \in x \rightarrow x_{1} \vdash \operatorname{coinduced} (f):= \operatorname{sInf} (\operatorname{ofPred} (\lambda b . \mathrm{Leq} (\operatorname{coinduced} (f , t), \operatorname{toTopologicalSpace} (b))))\in \mathrm{AddGroupTopology} (x_{1})$
French
Soit $f$ une fonction des éléments de $x$ à des éléments de $x_{1}$. Alors $\operatorname{coinduced}$ appliqué à $f$ est une topologie de groupes additive de $x_{1}$ défini comme l'infimum de $\operatorname{ofPred}$ appliqué à la fonction qui envoie $b$ à $\mathrm{Leq} (\operatorname{coinduced} (f , t), \operatorname{toTopologicalSpace} (b))$.

Mathlib.Topology.Algebra.Group.Neighborhood

nhdsAddHomdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Group.Neighborhood, function or constant
Wording
the neighbourhood filter additive homomorphism (from the name)
English
The neighbourhood filter additive homomorphism is an additive homomorphism of $G$ and the set of filters of $G$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $\mathrm{nhds}$ and the function that maps $x_{2}$ and $x_{3}$ to $\mathrm{nhds\_add}$ applied to $x_{2}$ and $x_{3}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{nhdsAddHom} := \operatorname{mk} (\mathrm{nhds} , \lambda x_{2} , x_{3} . \mathrm{nhds\_add} (x_{2} , x_{3}))\in \mathrm{AddHom} (G , \mathrm{Filter} (G))$
French
L'homomorphisme additif de filtres de voisinages est un homomorphisme additif de $G$ et l'ensemble de filtres de $G$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{nhds}$ et la fonction qui envoie $x_{2}$ et $x_{3}$ à $\mathrm{nhds\_add}$ appliqué à $x_{2}$ et $x_{3}$.

Mathlib.Topology.Algebra.Group.TopologicalAbelianization

TopologicalAbelianizationdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Group.TopologicalAbelianization, type
Wording
topological abelianization of G (from the name)
English
Let $G$ be a set. Then a topological abelianization of $G$ is an element of the function that maps $G$ to the set of quotients of $G$ and the topological closure of the commutator subgroup of $G$.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{TopologicalAbelianization} (G):= \lambda G . \operatorname{Quotient} (G , \operatorname{topologicalClosure} (\mathrm{commutator} (G)))$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors une abélianisation topologique de $G$ est un élément de la fonction qui envoie $G$ à l'ensemble de quotients de $G$ et la clôture topologique du sous-groupe de commutateurs de $G$.

Wording
the point reflection of p (from the name)
English
Let $p$ be an element of $P$. Then the point reflection of $p$ is a homeomorphism of $P$ and $P$ defined as the composition of the constant difference of $p$ and the vector sum constant of $p$.

$p \in P \vdash \operatorname{pointReflection} (p):= \operatorname{trans} (\operatorname{constVSub} (p), \operatorname{vaddConst} (p))\in \mathrm{Homeomorph} (P , P)$

Mathlib.Topology.Algebra.Group.Units

ContinuousAddEquiv.piAddUnitsdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Group.Units, function or constant
Wording
the product additive unit (from the name)
English
The product additive unit is a continuous additive equivalence of the set of additive units of the set of functions from elements $i$ of $x$ to elements of $M$ applied to $i$ and the set of functions from elements $i_{1}$ of $x$ to additive units of $M$ applied to $i_{1}$.
Formula
$\operatorname{piAddUnits} \in \mathrm{ContinuousAddEquiv} (\mathrm{AddUnits} (x \rightarrow M (i)), x \rightarrow \mathrm{AddUnits} (M (i_{1})))$
French
L'unité additive de produits est une équivalence additive continue de l'ensemble d'unités additives de l'ensemble de fonctions des éléments $i$ de $x$ à des éléments de $M$ appliqué à $i$ et l'ensemble de fonctions des éléments $i_{1}$ de $x$ à des unités additives de $M$ appliqué à $i_{1}$.
ContinuousAddEquiv.sumAddUnitsdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Group.Units, function or constant
Wording
the sum additive unit of M and N (from the name)
English
Let $M$ and $N$ be sets. Then the sum additive unit of $M$ and $N$ is a continuous additive equivalence of the set of additive units of $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $M$ and $N$ and $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to the set of additive units of $M$ and the set of additive units of $N$.
Formula

Mathlib.Topology.Algebra.IsUniformGroup.Basic

AddOpposite.opUniformEquivLeftdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.IsUniformGroup.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the left opposite uniform equivalence of G (from the name)
English
Let $G$ be a set. Then the left opposite uniform equivalence of $G$ is a uniform equivalence of $G$ and the set of additive opposites of $G$.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{opUniformEquivLeft} (G)\in \mathrm{UniformEquiv} (G , \mathrm{AddOpposite} (G))$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors l'équivalence uniforme opposée gauche de $G$ est une équivalence uniforme de $G$ et l'ensemble d'opposés additifs de $G$.

ClosedSubmodule.mapEquivdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ClosedSubmodule, function or constant

the map equivalence of f (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a continuous linear equivalence of $M$ and $N$. Then the map equivalence of $f$ is an equivalence from the set of closed submodules of $R$ and $M$ to the set of closed submodules of $R$ and $N$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearEquiv} (\operatorname{id} (R), M , N)\vdash \operatorname{mapEquiv} (f)\in \EquivMACRoAL {\mathrm{ClosedSubmodule} (R , M)}{\mathrm{ClosedSubmodule} (R , N)}$
French
Soit $f$ une équivalence linéaire continue de $M$ et $N$. Alors l'équivalence d'applications de $f$ est une équivalence de l'ensemble de sous-modules fermés de $R$ et $M$ à l'ensemble de sous-modules fermés de $R$ et $N$.
ClosedSubmodule.toClosedsdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ClosedSubmodule, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be a closed submodule of $R$ and $M$. Then $\operatorname{toCloseds}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$ is an element of $\operatorname{Closeds}$ applied to $M$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to the carrier of $\operatorname{toAddSubsemigroup}$ applied to $\operatorname{toAddSubmonoid}$ applied to $\operatorname{toSubmodule}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$ and $\operatorname{isClosed'}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{self} \in \mathrm{ClosedSubmodule} (R , M)\vdash \operatorname{toCloseds} (\mathrm{self}):= \operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{carrier} (\operatorname{toAddSubsemigroup} (\operatorname{toAddSubmonoid} (\operatorname{toSubmodule} (\mathrm{self})))), \operatorname{isClosed'} (\mathrm{self}))\in \operatorname{Closeds} (M)$
French
Soit $\mathrm{self}$ un sous-module fermé de $R$ et $M$. Alors $\operatorname{toCloseds}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$ est un élément de $\operatorname{Closeds}$ appliqué à $M$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à l'ensemble sous-jacent de $\operatorname{toAddSubsemigroup}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toAddSubmonoid}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toSubmodule}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$ et $\operatorname{isClosed'}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$.
Submodule.closuredefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ClosedSubmodule, function or constant
Wording
the closure of s (from the docstring)
English
Let $s$ be a submodule of $R$ and $M$. Then the closure of $s$ is a closed submodule of $R$ and $M$.
Formula
$s \in \mathrm{Submodule} (R , M)\vdash \operatorname{closure} (s)\in \mathrm{ClosedSubmodule} (R , M)$
French
Soit $s$ un sous-module de $R$ et $M$. Alors la clôture de $s$ est un sous-module fermé de $R$ et $M$.

ContinuousLinearMap.ofIsTopCompldefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.Complement, function or constant
Wording
the continuous linear map of x7 and x8 (from the name)
English
Assume that $p$ is a top complement with respect to $q$. Let $x_{7}$ be a continuous linear map of $\SubtypeSet {x_{10}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {p}{x_{10}})}$ and $F$. Let $x_{8}$ be a continuous linear map of $\SubtypeSet {x_{9}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {q}{x_{9}})}$ and $F$. Then the continuous linear map of $x_{7}$ and $x_{8}$ is a continuous linear map of $E$ and $F$ defined as the composition of the coproduct of $x_{7}$ and $x_{8}$ and the continuous linear map of the inverse of the product equivalence of $p$ and $q$.
Formula
$\operatorname{IsTopCompl} (p , q), x_{7} \in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), \SubtypeSet {x_{10}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {p}{x_{10}})}, F), x_{8} \in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), \SubtypeSet {x_{9}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {q}{x_{9}})}, F)\vdash \operatorname{ofIsTopCompl} (h_{2} , x_{7} , x_{8}):= \operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{coprod} (x_{7} , x_{8}), \operatorname{toContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{symm} (\operatorname{prodEquivOfIsTopCompl} (p , q , h_{2}))))\in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), E , F)$

Submodule.ClosedComplementeddefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.Complement, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $p$ be a submodule of $R$ and $M$. Then $\operatorname{ClosedComplemented}$ holds for $p$, if there exists a continuous linear map $f$ of $M$ and $\SubtypeSet {x_{7}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {p}{x_{7}})}$, such that for all elements $x_{4}$ of $\SubtypeSet {x_{6}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {p}{x_{6}})}$, $\EqtMACRoH {f (x_{4})}{x_{4}}$.
Formula
$p \in \mathrm{Submodule} (R , M)\vdash \operatorname{ClosedComplemented} (p):\Leftrightarrow \exists f \in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), M , \SubtypeSet {x_{7}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {p}{x_{7}})}). \forall x_{4} \in \SubtypeSet {x_{6}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {p}{x_{6}})}. \EqtMACRoJ {f (x_{4})}{x_{4}}$
French
Soit $p$ un sous-module de $R$ et $M$. Alors $\operatorname{ClosedComplemented}$ tient pour $p$ s'il existe une application linéaire continue $f$ de $M$ et $\SubtypeSet {x_{7}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {p}{x_{7}})}$ tel que pour tous les éléments $x_{4}$ de $\SubtypeSet {x_{6}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {p}{x_{6}})}$, $\EqtMACRoH {f (x_{4})}{x_{4}}$.
Submodule.ClosedComplemented.complementdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.Complement, function or constant
Wording
the complement of h (from the name)
English
Assume that $\operatorname{ClosedComplemented}$ holds for $p$. Then the complement of $h$ is a submodule of $R$ and $M$ defined as $\operatorname{choose}$ applied to $\mathrm{Submodule\_\_ClosedComplemented\_\_exists\_isTopCompl}$ applied to $h$.
Formula
$\operatorname{ClosedComplemented} (p)\vdash \operatorname{complement} (h):= \operatorname{choose} (\mathrm{Submodule\_\_ClosedComplemented\_\_exists\_isTopCompl} (h))\in \mathrm{Submodule} (R , M)$
French
Supposons que $\operatorname{ClosedComplemented}$ tient pour $p$. Alors le complément de $h$ est un sous-module de $R$ et $M$ défini comme $\operatorname{choose}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{Submodule\_\_ClosedComplemented\_\_exists\_isTopCompl}$ appliqué à $h$.

Wording

Let $p$ and $q$ be submodules of $R$ and $M$ such that $p$ is a top complement with respect to $q$. Then the product equivalence of $p$ and $q$ is a continuous linear equivalence of $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $\SubtypeSet {x_{4}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {p}{x_{4}})}$ and $\SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {q}{x_{5}})}$ and $M$.

$p , q \in \mathrm{Submodule} (R , M), \operatorname{IsTopCompl} (p , q)\vdash \operatorname{prodEquivOfIsTopCompl} (p , q , h_{1})\in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearEquiv} (\operatorname{id} (R), \mathrm{Prod} (\SubtypeSet {x_{4}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {p}{x_{4}})}, \SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {q}{x_{5}})}), M)$

Submodule.projectionLdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.Complement, function or constant
Wording
the projection of p and q (from the name)
English
Let $p$ and $q$ be submodules of $R$ and $M$ such that $p$ is a top complement with respect to $q$. Then the projection of $p$ and $q$ is a continuous linear map of $M$ and $M$ defined as the composition of the subtype of $p$ and $\operatorname{projectionOntoL}$ applied to $p$, $q$ and $h$.
Formula
$p , q \in \mathrm{Submodule} (R , M), \operatorname{IsTopCompl} (p , q)\vdash \operatorname{projectionL} (p , q , h):= \operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{subtypeL} (p), \operatorname{projectionOntoL} (p , q , h))\in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), M , M)$
